Chapter 73 · 第七十三章

Courage in daring kills; courage in not daring lives. Of the two, one benefits, one harms. What heaven hates, who knows the reason? Thus the sage treats it as difficult. The Way of heaven contends without fighting, responds without speaking, summons without calling, plans unhurriedly. Heaven's net is vast; its mesh is wide, yet nothing slips through.

勇于敢则杀，勇于不敢则活。两者或利或害。天之所恶，孰知其故？天之道，不战而善胜，不言而善应，不召而自来，弹而善谋。天网恢恢，疏而不失。

Chapter 74 · 第七十四章

If the people do not fear death, how can death frighten them? If they always feared death, and the wicked were seized and killed, who would dare? There is always a lord of execution. To kill in his place is like cutting wood in the master carpenter's place; few who cut in the carpenter's place do not injure their hands.

若民恒且不畏死，奈何以杀惧之也？若民恒且畏死，而为奇者，吾得执而杀之，夫孰敢矣？若民恒且必畏死，则恒有司杀者。夫伐司杀者杀，是伐大匠斫也。夫伐大匠斫者，则希不伤其手矣。

Chapter 75 · 第七十五章

The people starve because their rulers levy heavy taxes — that is why they starve. The people are hard to govern because their rulers interfere — that is why they are hard to govern. The people risk death because their rulers cling to life — that is why they risk it. One who does not cling to life is worthier than one who prizes living.

人之饥也，以其取食税之多也，是以饥。百姓之不治也，以其上有以为也，是以不治。民之轻死也，以其求生之厚也，是以轻死。夫唯无以生为者，是贤贵生。

Chapter 76 · 第七十六章

In life, man is soft and supple; in death, rigid. All things — grass and trees — are soft and tender in life, withered and dry in death. Thus the hard and strong belong to death; the soft and weak belong to life. Therefore strong weapons do not win; strong trees are felled. The strong and great take the lower place; the soft and weak the higher.

人之生也柔弱，其死也恒仞坚强。万物草木之生也柔脆，其死也枯槁。故曰：坚强者，死之徒也；柔弱微细者，生之徒也。兵强则不胜，木强则竞。故强大居下，柔弱微细居上。

Chapter 77 · 第七十七章

The Way of heaven — is it not like a drawn bow? What is high it presses down; what is low it lifts; what has excess it diminishes; what lacks it supplies. The Way of heaven diminishes the excessive and supplements the lacking. The way of man is otherwise: it takes from the lacking to serve the excessive. Who can use excess to serve the world? Only one with the Way. Thus the sage acts without claiming, succeeds without dwelling; he does not wish to display his worth.

天之道，其犹张弓与？高者抑之，下者举之；有余者损之，不足者补之。天之道，损有余而补不足。人之道则不然，损不足而奉有余。孰能有余以奉天下？唯有道者。是以圣人为而弗有，成功而弗居也，若此其不欲见贤也。

Chapter 78 · 第七十八章

Nothing under heaven is softer than water, yet nothing conquers the hard as water does; nothing can change it. The weak overcome the strong, the soft overcome the hard — none under heaven know this, none can practice it. Thus the sage says: to bear the state's filth is to be lord of the land; to bear the state's misfortune is to be king of the world. Straight words sound paradoxical.

天下莫柔弱于水，而攻坚强者莫之能先也，以其无以易之也。故柔之胜刚也，弱之胜强也，天下莫不知，而莫能行也。故圣人之言云：受国之诟，是谓社稷之主；受国之不祥，是谓天下之王。正言若反。

Chapter 79 · 第七十九章

To settle a great grievance, some resentment always remains; how can this be called good? Thus the sage holds the left half of the contract and presses no one. The virtuous keep the contract; the virtuousless pursue collection. The Way of heaven has no favorites; it is always with the good person.

和大怨，必有余怨，焉可以为善？是以圣人执左契而不以责于人。故有德司契，无德司彻。夫天道无亲，恒与善人。

Chapter 80 · 第八十章

Let the state be small with few people. Though tools exist ten- and hundredfold, they are unused; the people value their lives and do not migrate far. Boats and carriages exist, but none ride them; armor and weapons exist, but none display them. Return to knotted cords in place of writing. Relish your food, admire your clothes, rest in your homes, delight in your customs. Neighboring states see each other, hear each other's chickens and dogs, yet the people grow old and die without visiting one another.

小邦寡民。使什佰人之器毋用，使民重死而远徙。有车舟无所乘之，有甲兵无所陈之，使民复结绳而用之。甘其食，美其服，乐其俗，安其居。邻邦相望，鸡犬之声相闻，民至老死不相往来。

Chapter 81 · 第八十一章

True words are not beautiful; beautiful words are not true. The good do not argue; those who argue are not good. The wise are not learned; the learned are not wise. The sage does not hoard; the more he does for others, the more he has; the more he gives, the richer he is. The Way of heaven benefits without harming; the Way of the sage acts without contending.

信言不美，美言不信。知言不博，博言不知。善者不多，多者不善。圣人无积，既以为人，己愈有；既以予人矣，己愈多。故天之道，利而不害；圣人之道，为而弗争。

AI&Philosophy January 15, 2026

On Practice

Mao Zedong's essay "On Practice" (实践论), written in July 1937, presented in full in bilingual layout — English on the left, Chinese on the right.

By Mao Zedong
I

The materialism of pre-Marxist times, in observing the problem of knowledge, left human society and the historical development of man out of account; it was therefore incapable of understanding the dependence of knowledge on social practice, that is, on production and the class struggle.

马克思以前的唯物论，离开人的社会性，离开人的历史发展，去观察认识问题，因此不能了解认识对社会实践的依赖关系，即认识对生产和阶级斗争的依赖关系。

II

First of all, Marxists regard man's activity in production as the most fundamental practical activity, the one that determines all other activities. Man's knowledge depends mainly on his activity in material production, through which he comes to understand natural phenomena, the properties of nature, the laws of nature, and the relations between man and nature; and through production activity he also gradually comes to understand, in varying degrees, certain relations between man and man. None of this knowledge can be obtained apart from activity in production.

首先，马克思主义者认为人类的生产活动是最基本的实践活动，是决定其他一切活动的东西。人的认识，主要地依赖于物质的生产活动，逐渐地了解自然的现象、自然的性质、自然的规律性、人和自然的关系；而且经过生产活动，也在各种不同程度上逐渐地认识了人和人的一定的相互关系。一切这些知识，离开生产活动是不能得到的。

III

In a classless society, each person, as a member of society, joins in cooperative effort with the other members, enters into definite relations of production with them, and engages in production to meet man's material needs. In class society, the members of the different social classes also enter into definite relations of production with each other in different ways, and engage in production to meet their material needs. This is the fundamental source from which human knowledge develops.

在没有阶级的社会中，每个人以社会一员的资格，同其他社会成员协力，结成一定的生产关系，从事生产活动，以解决人类物质生活问题。在各种阶级的社会中，各阶级的社会成员，则又以各种不同的方式，结成一定的生产关系，从事生产活动，以解决人类物质生活问题。这是人的认识发展的基本来源。

IV

Man's social practice is not confined to activity in production, but takes many other forms — class struggle, political life, scientific and artistic pursuits; in short, man participates in all spheres of social life. Thus man's knowledge, in addition to material life, also derives from political and cultural life (both of which are intimately bound up with material life), in which he comes to understand, in varying degrees, the various relations between man and man. Among these, the class struggle in its various forms exercises a particularly deep influence on the development of man's knowledge. In class society, everyone lives as a member of a particular class, and every kind of thinking, without exception, is stamped with the brand of a class.

人的社会实践，不限于生产活动一种形式，还有多种其他的形式，阶级斗争，政治生活，科学和艺术的活动，总之社会实际生活的一切领域都是社会的人所参加的。因此，人的认识，在物质生活以外，还从政治生活文化生活中（与物质生活密切联系），在各种不同程度上，知道人和人的各种关系。其中，尤以各种形式的阶级斗争，给予人的认识发展以深刻的影响。在阶级社会中，每一个人都在一定的阶级地位中生活，各种思想无不打上阶级的烙印。

V

Marxists hold that man's activity in production is the most fundamental practical activity and develops step by step from a lower to a higher level. Consequently, man's knowledge, whether of nature or of society, also develops step by step from a lower to a higher level, that is, from the shallow to the deep, from the one-sided to the many-sided. For a very long period in history, men's understanding of society was confined to the one-sided view; this was due, on the one hand, to the bias of the exploiting classes, which constantly distorted the history of society, and on the other, to the small scale of production, which limited men's outlook. It was not until the modern proletariat emerged together with enormous productive forces — large-scale industry — that men could achieve a comprehensive and historical understanding of the development of society and turn this knowledge into a science, the science of Marxism.

马克思主义者认为人类社会的生产活动，是一步又一步地由低级向高级发展，因此，人们的认识，不论对于自然界方面，对于社会方面，也都是一步又一步地由低级向高级发展，即由浅入深，由片面到更多的方面。在很长的历史时期内，大家对于社会的历史只能限于片面的了解，这一方面是由于剥削阶级的偏见经常歪曲社会的历史，另方面，则由于生产规模的狭小，限制了人们的眼界。人们能够对于社会历史的发展作全面的历史的了解，把对于社会的认识变成了科学，这只是到了伴随巨大生产力——大工业而出现近代无产阶级的时候，这就是马克思主义的科学。

VI

Marxists hold that man's social practice alone is the criterion of the truth of his knowledge of the external world. What actually happens is this: man's knowledge is verified only when, in the course of social practice (in the process of material production, the class struggle, or scientific experiment), he achieves the results he anticipated. If a man wants to succeed in his work, that is, to achieve the results he anticipated, he must bring his ideas into correspondence with the laws of the objective world; if they do not correspond, he will fail in practice. After he fails, he learns from his failure and corrects his ideas to make them correspond to the laws of the external world, and thus he can turn failure into success. This is what is meant by "failure is the mother of success" and "a fall into the pit, a gain in your wit." The dialectical-materialist theory of knowledge places practice in the primary position, holding that human knowledge cannot be divorced from practice in the slightest degree, and repudiating all erroneous theories which deny the importance of practice or separate knowledge from practice. Lenin said: "Practice is higher than (theoretical) knowledge, for it has not only the character of universality, but also the character of immediate reality." The philosophy of Marxism — dialectical materialism — has two most conspicuous characteristics: one is its class nature, for it openly avows that dialectical materialism serves the proletariat; the other is its practical character, for it emphasizes the dependence of theory on practice, that the basis of theory is practice, and that theory in turn serves practice. Whether an idea or theory is true is not determined by subjective feelings, but by the objective result of social practice. The standard of truth can only be social practice. The viewpoint of practice is the primary and basic viewpoint of the dialectical-materialist theory of knowledge.

马克思主义者认为，只有人们的社会实践，才是人们对于外界认识的真理性的标准。实际的情形是这样的，只有在社会实践过程中（物质生产过程中，阶级斗争过程中，科学实验过程中），人们达到了思想中所预想的结果时，人们的认识才被证实了。人们要想得到工作的胜利即得到预想的结果，一定要使自己的思想合于客观外界的规律性，如果不合，就会在实践中失败。人们经过失败之后，也就从失败取得教训，改正自己的思想使之适合于外界的规律性，人们就能变失败为胜利，所谓"失败者成功之母"，"吃一堑长一智"，就是这个道理。辩证唯物论的认识论把实践提到第一的地位，认为人的认识一点也不能离开实践，排斥一切否认实践重要性、使认识离开实践的错误理论。列宁这样说过："实践高于（理论的）认识，因为它不但有普遍性的品格，而且还有直接现实性的品格。"马克思主义的哲学辩证唯物论有两个最显著的特点：一个是它的阶级性，公然申明辩证唯物论是为无产阶级服务的；再一个是它的实践性，强调理论对于实践的依赖关系，理论的基础是实践，又转过来为实践服务。判定认识或理论之是否真理，不是依主观上觉得如何而定，而是依客观上社会实践的结果如何而定。真理的标准只能是社会的实践。实践的观点是辩证唯物论的认识论之第一的和基本的观点。

VII

But how does man's knowledge arise from practice and in turn serve practice? This is made clear as soon as we examine the process of development of knowledge.

然而人的认识究竟怎样从实践发生，而又服务于实践呢？这只要看一看认识的发展过程就会明了的。

VIII

In the process of practice, man at first sees only the phenomenal side of things, sees them in isolation, one by one, and sees only their external relations. For instance, some people from outside come to Yenan on a fact-finding tour. In the first day or two they see its topography, streets, and houses, meet many people, attend banquets, evening parties, and mass meetings, hear various talks, and read various documents — all these being the phenomena, the individual sides, and the external relations of things. This stage of cognition, in which one perceives through the senses, is called the perceptual stage of knowledge, the stage of sensations and impressions.

原来人在实践过程中，开始只是看到过程中各个事物的现象方面，看到各个事物的片面，看到各个事物之间的外部联系。例如有些外面的人们到延安来考察，头一二天，他们看到了延安的地形、街道、屋宇，接触了许多的人，参加了宴会、晚会和群众大会，听到了各种说话，看到了各种文件，这些就是事物的现象，事物的各个片面以及这些事物的外部联系。这叫做认识的感性阶段，就是感觉和印象的阶段。

IX

As social practice continues, the things which give rise to sensations and impressions in man in the course of practice are repeated many times; then a leap occurs in the brain in the process of cognition, and concepts are formed. Concepts are no longer the phenomena of things, no longer the individual sides of things, no longer their external relations; they grasp the essence, the totality, and the internal relations of things. Between concept and sensation there is not merely a difference of degree, but a difference of kind. Proceeding further, by means of judgment and inference, one can arrive at logical conclusions. The expression in Romance of the Three Kingdoms, "a frown gives away a plan," and in ordinary speech, "let me think it over," refer to man's use of concepts in his brain to form judgments and inferences. This is the second stage of knowledge. The stage of concepts, judgments, and inferences is the more important stage in the entire process of a man's knowledge of a thing; it is the stage of rational knowledge. The real task of knowledge lies in arriving, through sensations, at thought, at step-by-step understanding of the internal contradictions of objective things, of their laws, and of the internal relations between this process and that — that is, arriving at logical knowledge. To repeat, the reason logical knowledge differs from perceptual knowledge is that perceptual knowledge pertains to the individual, the phenomenal, and the external relations of things, whereas logical knowledge takes a big stride forward, grasping the totality, the essence, and the internal relations of things, and exposing the internal contradictions of the surrounding world; thus it enables us to grasp the development of the surrounding world in its totality, in the internal relations of all its aspects.

社会实践的继续，使人们在实践中引起感觉和印象的东西反复了多次，于是在人们的脑子里生起了一个认识过程中的突变（即飞跃），产生了概念。概念这种东西已经不是事物的现象，不是事物的各个片面，不是它们的外部联系，而是抓着了事物的本质，事物的全体，事物的内部联系了。概念同感觉，不但是数量上的差别，而且有了性质上的差别。循此继进，使用判断和推理的方法，就可产生出合乎论理的结论来。《三国演义》上所谓"眉头一皱计上心来"，我们普通说话所谓"让我想一想"，就是人在脑子中运用概念以作判断和推理的工夫。这是认识的第二个阶段。外来的考察人们……这个概念、判断和推理的阶段，在人们对于一个事物的整个认识过程中是更重要的阶段，也就是理性认识的阶段。认识的真正任务在于经过感觉而到达于思维，到达于逐步了解客观事物的内部矛盾，了解它的规律性，了解这一过程和那一过程间的内部联系，即到达于论理的认识。重复地说，论理的认识所以和感性的认识不同，是因为感性的认识是属于事物之片面的、现象的、外部联系的东西，论理的认识则推进了一大步，到达了事物的全体的、本质的、内部联系的东西，到达了暴露周围世界的内在的矛盾，因而能在周围世界的总体上，在周围世界一切方面的内部联系上去把握周围世界的发展。

X

This dialectical-materialist theory of the process of development of knowledge, based on practice and proceeding from the shallow to the deep, was never worked out by anyone before Marx. Marxism, for the first time, correctly solved this problem, pointing out materially and dialectically the deepening movement of knowledge — the movement, by men in social practice, from perceptual to rational knowledge, in their complex and repeatedly recurring practices of production and class struggle. Lenin said: "The abstraction of matter, of a law of nature, of value, and so on — in a word, all scientific (correct, serious, not absurd) abstractions reflect nature more deeply, truly, and completely." Marxism-Leninism holds that the two stages in the process of cognition have different characteristics: at the lower stage, cognition appears as perceptual; at the higher stage, as rational; but both are stages in a single process of cognition. Perceptual knowledge and rational knowledge are different in character, yet they are not separate from each other; they are unified on the basis of practice. Our practice proves that what is perceived cannot be understood immediately, and that only what is understood can be perceived more deeply. Sensation solves only the problem of phenomena; theory alone can solve the problem of essence. The solution of these problems cannot be separated from practice in the slightest degree. Whoever wants to know a thing has no way of doing so except by coming into contact with it, that is, living (practicing) in its environment. One cannot know in feudal society beforehand the laws of capitalist society, because capitalism has not yet emerged and there is no such practice. Marxism could only be the product of capitalist society. Marx could not in the era of free capitalism have known concretely beforehand the specific laws of the imperialist era, because imperialism, the final stage of capitalism, had not yet arrived and there was no such practice; only Lenin and Stalin could undertake this task. Apart from their genius, the reason Marx, Engels, Lenin, and Stalin could work out their theories was mainly that they personally took part in the practice of the class struggle and scientific experiment of their time; without this latter condition, no genius could have succeeded. "The scholar who never leaves his gate knows all the affairs under heaven" was an empty phrase in ancient times when technology was undeveloped; although in the modern era of developed technology one can realize this saying, those who really possess first-hand knowledge are the people engaged in practice throughout the world. Such people acquire their knowledge in practice, and it reaches "the scholar who never leaves his gate" through written and technological media, so that he can "know all the affairs under heaven" indirectly. If you want to know a certain thing or certain things directly, you have no way to do so except by personally taking part in the practical struggle to change reality, to change that thing or those things; only thus can you come into contact with the phenomena of that thing or those things, and only through personal participation in the practical struggle to change reality can you expose the essence of that thing or those things and understand them. This is the path of knowledge actually followed by everyone, though some people go wrong, or take a wrong path. If you want knowledge, you must take part in the practice of changing reality. If you want to know the taste of a pear, you must change the pear — eat it yourself. If you want to know the structure and properties of the atom, you must make physical and chemical experiments, changing the condition of the atom. If you want to know the theory and methods of revolution, you must take part in revolution. All genuine knowledge originates in direct experience. But one cannot have direct experience of everything; in fact, most of our knowledge is indirect experience, that is, all the knowledge of ancient times and foreign lands. Such knowledge is direct experience for our ancestors and for foreigners; if this knowledge, when it was direct experience for them, met the condition Lenin referred to — "scientific abstraction" — and scientifically reflected objective things, then this knowledge is reliable, otherwise it is unreliable. Thus a man's knowledge consists of two parts: direct experience and indirect experience. Moreover, what is indirect experience for me is still direct experience for others. Therefore, taken as a whole, knowledge of any kind cannot be separated from direct experience. The source of all knowledge lies in man's sensations of the objective external world through his sense organs; anyone who denies this sensation, denies direct experience, or denies personal participation in the practical struggle to change reality, is not a materialist. The reason the "expert" is ridiculous lies in just this place. The Chinese have an old saying: "If you do not enter the tiger's den, how can you catch the tiger's cub?" This saying is true for man's practice, and true also for the theory of knowledge. Knowledge divorced from practice is impossible.

这种基于实践的由浅入深的辩证唯物论的关于认识发展过程的理论，在马克思主义以前，是没有一个人这样解决过的。马克思主义的唯物论，第一次正确地解决了这个问题，唯物地而且辩证地指出了认识的深化的运动，指出了社会的人在他们的生产和阶级斗争的复杂的、经常反复的实践中，由感性认识到论理认识的推移的运动。列宁说过："物质的抽象，自然规律的抽象，价值的抽象以及其他等等，一句话，一切科学的（正确的、郑重的、非瞎说的）抽象，都更深刻、更正确、更完全地反映着自然。"马克思列宁主义认为：认识过程中两个阶段的特性，在低级阶段，认识表现为感性的，在高级阶段，认识表现为论理的，但任何阶段，都是统一的认识过程中的阶段。感性和理性二者的性质不同，但又不是互相分离的，它们在实践的基础上统一起来了。我们的实践证明：感觉到了的东西，我们不能立刻理解它，只有理解了的东西才更深刻地感觉它。感觉只解决现象问题，理论才解决本质问题。这些问题的解决，一点也不能离开实践。无论何人要认识什么事物，除了同那个事物接触，即生活于（实践于）那个事物的环境中，是没有法子解决的。不能在封建社会就预先认识资本主义社会的规律，因为资本主义还未出现，还无这种实践。马克思主义只能是资本主义社会的产物。马克思不能在自由资本主义时代就预先具体地认识帝国主义时代的某些特异的规律，因为帝国主义这个资本主义最后阶段还未到来，还无这种实践，只有列宁和斯大林才能担当此项任务。马克思、恩格斯、列宁、斯大林之所以能够作出他们的理论，除了他们的天才条件之外，主要地是他们亲自参加了当时的阶级斗争和科学实验的实践，没有这后一个条件，任何天才也是不能成功的。"秀才不出门，全知天下事"，在技术不发达的古代只是一句空话，在技术发达的现代虽然可以实现这句话，然而真正亲知的是天下实践着的人，那些人在他们的实践中间取得了"知"，经过文字和技术的传达而到达于"秀才"之手，秀才乃能间接地"知天下事"。如果要直接地认识某种或某些事物，便只有亲身参加于变革现实、变革某种或某些事物的实践的斗争中，才能触到那种或那些事物的现象，也只有在亲身参加变革现实的实践的斗争中，才能暴露那种或那些事物的本质而理解它们。这是任何人实际上走着的认识路程，不过有些人走错了路，或者走了弯路罢了。你要有知识，你就得参加变革现实的实践。你要知道梨子的滋味，你就得变革梨子，亲口吃一吃。你要知道原子的组织同性质，你就得实行物理学和化学的实验，变革原子的情况。你要知道革命的理论和方法，你就得参加革命。一切真知都是从直接经验发源的。但人不能事事直接经验，事实上多数的知识都是间接经验的东西，这就是一切古代的和外域的知识。这些知识在古人在外人是直接经验的东西，如果在古人外人直接经验时是符合于列宁所说的条件"科学的抽象"，是科学地反映了客观的事物，那末这些知识是可靠的，否则就是不可靠的。所以，一个人的知识，不外直接经验的和间接经验的两部分。而且在我为间接经验者，在人则仍为直接经验。因此，就知识的总体说来，无论何种知识都是不能离开直接经验的。任何知识的来源，在于人的肉体感官对客观外界的感觉，否认了这个感觉，否认了直接经验，否认亲自参加变革现实的实践，他就不是唯物论者。"知识里手"之所以可笑，原因就是在这个地方。中国人有一句老话："不入虎穴，焉得虎子。"这句话对于人们的实践是真理，对于认识论也是真理。离开实践的认识是不可能的。

XI

In order to make clear the dialectical-materialist movement of cognition — the movement of gradually deepening knowledge — arising on the basis of the practice of changing reality, let us give a few further concrete examples.

为了明了基于变革现实的实践而产生的辩证唯物论的认识运动——认识的逐渐深化的运动，下面再举出几个具体的例子。

XII

The proletariat's understanding of capitalist society went through a similar process. In the early period of its practice — the period of machine-breaking and spontaneous struggle — the proletariat was still at the stage of perceptual knowledge, understanding only the individual aspects and external relations of the various phenomena of capitalism. At that time it was still a "class in itself." But when it reached the second period of its practice — the period of conscious and organized economic and political struggle — as a result of practice, of the long experience of struggle, when Marx and Engels, the scientific method, summed up this experience and produced the theory of Marxism, which was used to educate the proletariat, the proletariat came to understand the essence of capitalist society, the exploitative relations between social classes, and the historical task of the proletariat, and became a "class for itself."

无产阶级对于资本主义社会的认识，在其实践的初期——破坏机器和自发斗争时期，他们还只在感性认识的阶段，只认识资本主义各个现象的片面及其外部的联系。这时，他们还是一个所谓"自在的阶级"。但是到了他们实践的第二个时期——有意识有组织的经济斗争和政治斗争的时期，由于实践，由于长期斗争的经验，经过马克思、恩格斯用科学的方法把这种种经验总结起来，产生了马克思主义的理论，用以教育无产阶级，这样就使无产阶级理解了资本主义社会的本质，理解了社会阶级的剥削关系，理解了无产阶级的历史任务，这时他们就变成了一个"自为的阶级"。

XIII

The same is true of the Chinese people's understanding of imperialism. The first stage was one of superficial, perceptual knowledge, as shown in the indiscriminate anti-foreign struggles of the Taiping Heavenly Kingdom movement and the Yihetuan movement. It was only in the second stage that the Chinese people advanced to rational knowledge, seeing the internal and external contradictions of imperialism, and realizing that imperialism, in alliance with the Chinese comprador class and the feudal class, was squeezing the Chinese people — an understanding that began around the May Fourth Movement of 1919.

中国人民对于帝国主义的认识也是这样。第一阶段是表面的感性的认识阶段，表现在太平天国运动和义和团运动等笼统的排外主义的斗争上。第二阶段才进到理性的认识阶段，看出了帝国主义内部和外部的各种矛盾，并看出了帝国主义联合中国买办阶级和封建阶级以压榨中国人民大众的实质，这种认识是从一九一九年五四运动前后才开始的。

XIV

Let us look at war. If the leaders of a war have no experience of war, then at the outset they do not understand the profound laws governing that specific war (for example, our war of agrarian revolution of the past ten years). In the beginning they merely went through many battles, in most of which they were defeated. It was precisely this experience (the experience of victories, especially of defeats) that enabled them to understand the inner thing running through the whole war — the laws of that specific war — and to understand strategy and tactics, and so to guide the war with confidence. If, at that time, an inexperienced person were put in charge of directing the war, he would likewise have to suffer a number of defeats (that is, gain experience) before he could grasp the correct laws of the war.

我们再来看战争。战争的领导者，如果他们是一些没有战争经验的人，对于一个具体的战争（例如我们过去十年的土地革命战争）的深刻的指导规律，在开始阶段是不了解的。他们在开始阶段只是身历了许多作战的经验，而且败仗是打得很多的。然而由于这些经验（胜仗，特别是败仗的经验），使他们能够理解贯串整个战争的内部的东西，即那个具体战争的规律性，懂得了战略和战术，因而能够有把握地去指导战争。此时，如果改换一个无经验的人去指导，又会要在吃了一些败仗之后（有了经验之后）才能理会战争的正确的规律。

XV

We often hear comrades who are unwilling to accept a work assignment say, "I am not sure." Why are they not sure? Because they have no understanding of the content and circumstances of that work in their laws, or have never had any contact with that kind of work, or have had too little of it, and so cannot speak of the laws governing such work. But when they have been given a detailed analysis of the situation and circumstances of the work, they feel somewhat sure and are willing to undertake it. If such a person has gone through this work for a period of time, and has gained experience of it, and if he is a person willing to examine the situation conscientiously, not one who approaches it subjectively, one-sidedly, and superficially, then he can draw his own conclusions as to how the work should be done, and his courage to work can be greatly increased. Only if there is understanding of the laws of the external world can one have assurance and courage. The "assurance" and "courage" spoken of above are matters of understanding of the objective laws.

常常听到一些同志在不能勇敢接受工作任务时说出来的一句话：没有把握。为什么没有把握呢？因为他对于这项工作的内容和环境没有规律性的了解，或者他从来就没有接触过这类工作，或者接触得不多，因而无从谈到这类工作的规律性。及至把工作的情况和环境给以详细分析之后，他就觉得比较地有了把握，愿意去做这项工作。如果这个人在这项工作中经过了一个时期，他有了这项工作的经验了，而他又是一个肯虚心体察情况的人，不是一个主观地、片面地、表面地看问题的人，他就能够自己做出应该怎样做工作的结论，他的工作勇气也就可以大大地提高了。惟有对于外界有规律性的了解，才能有把握，才能有勇气。以上所谓有把握，所谓有勇气，都是就对于客观规律性的了解而言。

XVI

From this it can be seen that the process of cognition has two stages. The first is the stage of coming into contact with external things, of sensations; the second is the stage of synthesis and rearrangement of the material of sensations — of concepts, judgments, and inferences. Only when the material of sensation is very rich (not fragmentary and incomplete) and corresponding to reality (not illusory) can correct concepts and logical conclusions be formed on the basis of such material.

由此看来，认识的过程，第一步，是开始接触外界事情，属于感觉的阶段。第二步，是综合感觉的材料加以整理和改造，属于概念、判断和推理的阶段。只有感觉的材料十分丰富（不是零碎不全）和合于实际（不是错觉），才能根据这样的材料造出正确的概念和论理来。

XVII

Here two points must be emphatically brought out. The first, already stated above and here repeated, is the dependence of rational knowledge on perceptual knowledge. Anyone who thinks that rational knowledge can be divorced from perceptual knowledge is an idealist. In the history of philosophy there is a school of "rationalists" which admits only the reality of reason and not the reality of experience, holding that reason alone is dependable while experience is not; their mistake lies in reversing the facts. The reason rational things are dependable is precisely that they originate from sensation; otherwise rational things would become a river without a source or a tree without roots — something subjective, self-engendered, and unreliable. In the order of the process of cognition, perceptual experience comes first; and it is precisely because we emphasize the significance of social practice in the process of cognition that only social practice can cause man's knowledge to begin — that is, can cause him to obtain perceptual experience from the objective external world. A person who shuts his eyes and stops his ears and is completely cut off from the objective external world has no knowledge whatsoever. Knowledge begins with experience — this is the materialism of the theory of knowledge.

这里有两个要点必须着重指明。第一个，在前面已经说过的，这里再重复说一说，就是理性认识依赖于感性认识的问题。如果以为理性认识可以不从感性认识得来，他就是一个唯心论者。哲学史上有所谓"唯理论"一派，就是只承认理性的实在性，不承认经验的实在性，以为只有理性靠得住，而感觉的经验是靠不住的，这一派的错误在于颠倒了事实。理性的东西所以靠得住，正是由于它来源于感性，否则理性的东西就成了无源之水，无本之木，而只是主观自生的靠不住的东西了。从认识过程的秩序说来，感觉经验是第一的东西，我们强调社会实践在认识过程中的意义，就在于只有社会实践才能使人的认识开始发生，也就是从客观外界得到感觉经验。一个闭目塞听、同客观外界根本绝缘的人，是无所谓认识的。认识开始于经验——这就是认识论的唯物论。

XVIII

The second point is that knowledge needs to be deepened, that the perceptual stage of knowledge needs to be developed to the rational stage — this is the dialectics of the theory of knowledge. To think that knowledge can stop at the lower, perceptual stage, that only perceptual knowledge is dependable and rational knowledge is not, is to repeat the error of the "empiricists" in the history of philosophy. The error of this theory lies in failing to understand that, although the material of sensation is a reflection of certain truths of the objective external world (I am not speaking here of the idealist empiricism which confines experience to so-called introspection), it is nevertheless one-sided and superficial, a reflection that is incomplete, one that does not reflect the essence of things. To reflect a thing in its entirety, its essence, and its internal laws, one must go through the operation of thinking, subjecting the rich data of sensation to the process of discarding the crude and selecting the essential, eliminating the false and retaining the true, proceeding from the one to the other and from the outside to the inside, and forming a system of concepts and theories. It is only this transformed cognition, so long as it is scientifically wrought on the basis of practice, that is, as Lenin said, a deeper, more correct, and more complete reflection of objective things. The vulgar practitioner does not proceed in this way; he respects experience but looks down on theory, and consequently cannot have a view of the whole process of the objective situation, lacks a clear direction, and has no far-sighted vision, being content with petty successes and narrow insights. If such a person directs a revolution, he will lead it onto a path where it hits a wall.

第二是认识有待于深化，认识的感性阶段有待于发展到理性阶段——这就是认识论的辩证法。如果以为认识可以停顿在低级的感性的阶段，以为只有感性认识可靠，而理性认识是靠不住的，这便是重复了哲学史上的"经验论"的错误。这种理论的错误，在于不知道感觉材料固然是客观外界某些真实性的反映（我这里不来说经验只是所谓内省体验的那种唯心的经验论），但它们仅是片面的和表面的东西，这种反映是不完全的，是没有反映事物本质的。要完全地反映整个的事物，反映事物的本质，反映事物的内部规律性，就必须经过思考作用，将丰富的感觉材料加以去粗取精、去伪存真、由此及彼、由表及里的改造制作工夫，造成概念和理论的系统，就必须从感性认识跃进到理性认识。这种改造过的认识，不是更空虚了更不可靠了的认识，相反，只要是在认识过程中根据于实践基础而科学地改造过的东西，正如列宁所说乃是更深刻、更正确、更完全地反映客观事物的东西。庸俗的事务主义家不是这样，他们尊重经验而看轻理论，因而不能通观客观过程的全体，缺乏明确的方针，没有远大的前途，沾沾自喜于一得之功和一孔之见。这种人如果指导革命，就会引导革命走上碰壁的地步。

XIX

Rational knowledge depends on perceptual knowledge, and perceptual knowledge needs to be developed to rational knowledge — this is the dialectical-materialist theory of knowledge. Neither the "rationalism" nor the "empiricism" of philosophy understands the historical or dialectical character of knowledge; each, though it contains a one-sided truth, is wrong in regard to the theory of knowledge as a whole. The dialectical-materialist movement of knowledge from the perceptual to the rational is unique to a small process of cognition (for example, of a single thing or a single task); but the movement of cognition is by no means complete with rational knowledge. If the dialectical-materialist movement of cognition stopped at rational knowledge, it would have dealt with only half the problem — and, moreover, from the Marxist standpoint, with the less important half. Marxist philosophy holds that the important question is not merely to understand the laws of the objective world and thus be able to explain it, but to use this knowledge of the objective laws to actively change the world. In Marx's view, theory is important, and its importance is fully expressed in Lenin's statement: "Without revolutionary theory there can be no revolutionary movement." But Marxism values theory precisely, and only, because it can guide action. If there is a correct theory, but one merely talks about it, files it away, and does not put it into practice, then however good the theory, it is meaningless. Knowledge begins with practice, reaches theoretical knowledge through practice, and must then return to practice. The active function of knowledge is manifested not only in the active leap from perceptual to rational knowledge, but, what is more important, in the leap from rational knowledge to revolutionary practice. The knowledge that has grasped the laws of the world must be redirected to the practice of changing the world — applied to the practice of production, to revolutionary class struggle and national struggle, and to scientific experiment. This is the process of testing theory and developing theory, and the continuation of the whole process of cognition. The question whether theoretical things correspond to objective truth is not, nor can it be, completely solved in the movement of cognition from the perceptual to the rational described above. The only way to solve this problem completely is to redirect rational knowledge to social practice, apply theory to practice, and see whether it can achieve the results anticipated. The reason many natural-science theories are called truth is not only that the natural scientists who created them did so, but that they were later confirmed by scientific practice. The reason Marxism-Leninism is called truth is not only that Marx, Engels, Lenin, and Stalin scientifically worked out these theories, but that they have been confirmed by the subsequent practice of revolutionary class struggle and national struggle. Dialectical materialism is universally true precisely because no one's practice can escape from its scope. The history of human knowledge tells us that the truth of many theories is incomplete, and that this incompleteness is corrected through the test of practice. Many theories are erroneous, and the test of practice corrects their errors. The so-called practice is the criterion of truth; the so-called viewpoint of "life, practice should be the primary and basic viewpoint of the theory of knowledge" — the reason lies here. Stalin put it well: "Theory becomes aimless if it is not connected with revolutionary practice, just as practice gropes in the dark if it is not illuminated by revolutionary theory."

理性认识依赖于感性认识，感性认识有待于发展到理性认识，这就是辩证唯物论的认识论。哲学上的"唯理论"和"经验论"都不懂得认识的历史性或辩证性，虽然各有片面的真理，但在认识论的全体上则都是错误的。由感性到理性之辩证唯物论的认识运动，对于一个小的认识过程（例如对于一个事物或一件工作）来说，是认识运动的第一步，然而认识运动至此还没有完结。辩证唯物论的认识运动，如果只到理性认识为止，那末还只说到问题的一半。而且对于马克思主义的哲学说来，还只说到非十分重要的那一半。马克思主义的哲学认为十分重要的问题，不在于懂得了客观世界的规律性，因而能够解释世界，而在于拿了这种对于客观规律性的认识去能动地改造世界。在马克思主义看来，理论是重要的，它的重要性充分地表现在列宁说过的一句话："没有革命的理论，就不会有革命的运动。"然而马克思主义看重理论，正是，也仅仅是，因为它能够指导行动。如果有了正确的理论，只是把它空谈一阵，束之高阁，并不实行，那末，这种理论再好也是没有意义的。认识从实践始，经过实践得到了理论的认识，还须再回到实践去。认识的能动作用，不但表现于从感性的认识到理性的认识之能动的飞跃，更重要的还须表现于从理性的认识到革命的实践这一个飞跃。抓着了世界的规律性的认识，必须把它再回到改造世界的实践中去，再用到生产实践、革命的阶级斗争和民族斗争的实践以及科学实验的实践中去。这就是检验理论和发展理论的过程，是整个认识过程的继续。理论的东西之是否符合于客观真理性这个问题，在前面说的由感性到理性之认识运动中是没有完全解决的，也不能完全解决的。要完全地解决这个问题，只有把理性的认识再回到社会实践中去，应用理论于实践，看它是否能够达到预想的目的。许多自然科学理论之所以被称为真理，不但在于自然科学家们创立这些学说的时候，而且在于为尔后的科学实践所证实的时候。马克思列宁主义之所以被称为真理，也不但在于马克思、恩格斯、列宁、斯大林等人科学地构成这些学说的时候，而且在于为尔后革命的阶级斗争和民族斗争的实践所证实的时候。辩证唯物论之所以为普遍真理，在于经过无论什么人的实践都不能逃出它的范围。人类认识的历史告诉我们，许多理论的真理性是不完全的，经过实践的检验而纠正了它们的不完全性。许多理论是错误的，经过实践的检验而纠正其错误。所谓实践是真理的标准，所谓"生活、实践底观点，应该是认识论底首先的和基本的观点"，理由就在这个地方。斯大林说得好："理论若不和革命实践联系起来，就会变成无对象的理论，同样，实践若不以革命理论为指南，就会变成盲目的实践。"

XX

At this point, is the movement of cognition completed? Our answer is: it is completed, and yet it is not. When men in society throw themselves into the practice of changing a certain objective process (whether a natural process or a social process) at a certain stage of its development, as a result of the reflection of the objective process and the operation of their subjective initiative, their knowledge moves from the perceptual to the rational, and they produce ideas, theories, plans, or schemes which in general correspond to the laws of that objective process; they then apply these ideas, theories, plans, or schemes to the practice of the same objective process, and, if they achieve the anticipated results — that is, if in practice the anticipated ideas, theories, plans, or schemes are turned into fact, or are in the main turned into fact — then the movement of cognition of that particular process may be considered completed with regard to that process. However, so far as the process of advancement is concerned, the movement of human knowledge is not completed. Any process, whether natural or social, advances and develops because of its internal contradictions and struggles, and the movement of human knowledge should advance and develop accordingly. As regards social movements, the true revolutionary guide must not only be good at correcting his own ideas, theories, plans, and schemes when they are wrong, as already mentioned, but must also, when a certain objective process has already shifted from one stage of development to another, be good at making himself and all the people taking part in the revolution shift in their subjective understanding along with it — that is, must see to it that the new revolutionary tasks and new working programs are proposed to suit the change in circumstances. It often happens that changes in circumstances are very rapid during a revolutionary period; and if the knowledge of the revolutionaries cannot keep pace with these rapid changes, they will be unable to lead the revolution to victory.

说到这里，认识运动就算完成了吗？我们的答复是完成了，又没有完成。社会的人们投身于变革在某一发展阶段内的某一客观过程的实践中（不论是关于变革某一自然过程的实践，或变革某一社会过程的实践），由于客观过程的反映和主观能动性的作用，使得人们的认识由感性的推移到了理性的，造成了大体上相应于该客观过程的法则性的思想、理论、计划或方案，然后再应用这种思想、理论、计划或方案于该同一客观过程的实践，如果能够实现预想的目的，即将预定的思想、理论、计划、方案在该同一过程的实践中变为事实，或者大体上变为事实，那末，对于这一具体过程的认识运动算是完成了。然而，对于过程的推移而言，人们的认识运动是没有完成的。任何过程，不论是属于自然界的和属于社会的，由于内部的矛盾和斗争，都是向前推移向前发展的，人们的认识运动也应跟着推移和发展。依社会运动来说，真正的革命的指导者，不但在于当自己的思想、理论、计划、方案有错误时须得善于改正，如同上面已经说到的，而且在于当某一客观过程已经从某一发展阶段向另一发展阶段推移转变的时候，须得善于使自己和参加革命的一切人员在主观认识上也跟着推移转变，即是要使新的革命任务和新的工作方案的提出，适合于新的情况的变化。革命时期情况的变化是很急速的，如果革命党人的认识不能随之而急速变化，就不能引导革命走向胜利。

XXI

However, it often happens that thinking lags behind reality; this is because man's cognition is limited by many social conditions. We oppose the die-hards in the revolutionary ranks, whose thinking fails to advance with changing objective circumstances and has historically manifested itself as right opportunism. These people fail to see that the struggle of contradictions has already pushed the objective process forward, while their cognition is still at the old stage. All die-hard factions have this characteristic in their thinking. Their thinking is divorced from social practice; they cannot stand at the head of the social chariot and act as its guide; they merely trail behind, grumbling that the chariot goes too fast, and try to drag it back or turn it around.

然而思想落后于实际的事是常有的，这是因为人的认识受了许多社会条件的限制的缘故。我们反对革命队伍中的顽固派，他们的思想不能随变化了的客观情况而前进，在历史上表现为右倾机会主义。这些人看不出矛盾的斗争已将客观过程推向前进了，而他们的认识仍然停止在旧阶段。一切顽固党的思想都有这样的特征。他们的思想离开了社会的实践，他们不能站在社会车轮的前头充任向导的工作，他们只知跟在车子后面怨恨车子走得太快了，企图把它向后拉，开倒车。

XXII

We also oppose the "Left" phrase-mongers. Their thinking goes beyond a certain stage of development of the objective process; some take their illusions for truth, others try to realize in the present an ideal which has real possibility only in the future; they are divorced from the practice of the majority of the people at present, from present reality, and their actions manifest themselves as adventurism in practice.

我们也反对"左"翼空谈主义。他们的思想超过客观过程的一定发展阶段，有些把幻想看作真理，有些则把仅在将来有现实可能性的理想，勉强地放在现时来做，离开了当前大多数人的实践，离开了当前的现实性，在行动上表现为冒险主义。

XXIII

Idealism and mechanical materialism, opportunism and adventurism, are all characterized by the splitting of the subjective from the objective and the divorce of cognition from practice. Our conclusion is the concrete and historical unity of the subjective and the objective, of theory and practice, of knowledge and action, and opposition to all erroneous "Left" or Right ideas divorced from concrete history.

唯心论和机械唯物论，机会主义和冒险主义，都是以主观和客观相分裂，以认识和实践相脱离为特征的。我们的结论是主观和客观、理论和实践、知和行的具体的历史的统一，反对一切离开具体历史的"左"的或右的错误思想。

XXIV

With the development of society, the responsibility of correctly understanding and changing the world has historically fallen on the shoulders of the proletariat and its political party. This process of changing the world, determined on the basis of scientific cognition, has reached in the world, and in China, a historic moment never known before — the moment of overturning entirely the dark aspects of the world and of China and turning them into an unprecedented world of light. The struggle of the proletariat and the revolutionary people to change the world includes the fulfillment of the following tasks: to change the objective world and also to change their own subjective world — to change their cognitive faculties and the relations between the subjective world and the objective world. On the earth a part of this change has already been carried out — that is the Soviet Union. They are still promoting this process of change. The Chinese people and the people of the world are also beginning this process of change. The so-called objective world that is to be changed includes all those who oppose change; their transformation must pass through a stage of compulsion before it can enter the stage of conscious transformation. When the time comes for all mankind to consciously change themselves and change the world, that will be the era of world communism.

社会的发展到了今天的时代，正确地认识世界和改造世界的责任，已经历史地落在无产阶级及其政党的肩上。这种根据科学认识而定下来的改造世界的实践过程，在世界、在中国，均已到达了一个历史的时节——自有历史以来未曾有过的重大时节，这就是整个儿地推翻世界和中国的黑暗面，把它们转变过来成为前所未有的光明世界。无产阶级和革命人民改造世界的斗争，包括实现下述的任务：改造客观世界，也改造自己的主观世界——改造自己的认识能力，改造主观世界同客观世界的关系。地球上已经有一部分实行了这种改造，这就是苏联。他们还正在促进这种改造过程。中国人民和世界人民也正在开始这个改造过程。所谓被改造的客观世界，其中包括了一切反对改造的人们，他们的被改造，须要通过强迫的阶段，然后才能进入自觉的阶段。世界到了全人类都自觉地改造自己和改造世界的时候，那就是世界的共产主义时代。

XXV

Through practice, discover truth; and again through practice, confirm truth and develop truth. From perceptual knowledge, actively develop to rational knowledge; and from rational knowledge, actively guide revolutionary practice, changing both the subjective and the objective world. Practice, knowledge, again practice, again knowledge — this form recurs in endless cycles, and with each cycle the content of practice and knowledge rises to a higher level. This is the whole theory of knowledge of dialectical materialism, this is the dialectical-materialist unity of knowledge and action.

通过实践而发现真理，又通过实践而证实真理和发展真理。从感性认识而能动地发展到理性认识，又从理性认识而能动地指导革命实践，改造主观世界和客观世界。实践、认识、再实践、再认识，这种形式，循环往复以至无穷，而实践和认识之每一循环的内容，都比较地进到了高一级的程度。这就是辩证唯物论的全部认识论，这就是辩证唯物论的知行统一观。

AI&Philosophy January 15, 2026

On Contradiction

Mao Zedong's essay "On Contradiction" (矛盾论), written in August 1937, presented in full in bilingual layout — English on the left, Chinese on the right.

By Mao Zedong
I · 一、两种宇宙观

In the history of human knowledge there have always been two views on the law of development of the universe: the metaphysical view and the dialectical view. These two views form two opposite conceptions of the universe. Lenin said: "There are two basic (or two possible? or two commonly seen in history?) conceptions of development (evolution): development as decrease and increase, as repetition; and development as the unity of opposites (the division of a unity into mutually exclusive opposites and their reciprocal relation)." What Lenin referred to are these two different worldviews.

在人类认识史中，从来就有关于宇宙发展法则的两种见解，一种是形而上学的见解，一种是辩证法的见解，形成了互相对立的两种宇宙观。列宁说："对于发展（进化）所持的两种基本的（或两种可能的？或两种在历史上常见的？）观点是：（一）认为发展是减少和增加，是重复；（二）认为发展是对立的统一（统一物分成为两个互相排斥的对立，而两个对立又互相关联着）。"列宁说的就是这两种不同的宇宙观。

I (cont.) · 一、两种宇宙观

The dialectical conception of the universe arose in ancient times, both in China and in Europe. But the ancient dialectics was of a spontaneous, naive character; given the social and historical conditions of the time, it could not be a complete theory, and hence could not fully explain the universe, and was later supplanted by metaphysics. In Europe, the materialism of the early bourgeoisie was also metaphysical. It was only after the rise of large-scale industry and the proletariat in the nineteenth century that the revolutionary dialectical-materialist worldview of the proletariat, Marxist dialectical materialism, was created.

辩证法的宇宙观，不论在中国，在欧洲，在古代就产生了。但是古代的辩证法带着自发的朴素的性质，根据当时的社会历史条件，还不可能有完备的理论，因而不能完全解释宇宙，后来就被形而上学所代替。在欧洲，资产阶级初期的唯物论，也是形而上学的。只有在十九世纪出现了大工业和无产阶级的时候，才产生了无产阶级的革命的世界观——马克思主义的辩证唯物论。

I (cont.) · 一、两种宇宙观

The so-called metaphysical or vulgar-evolutionist view of the world regards things as isolated, static, and one-sided, viewing all things in the world, their forms and their species, as isolatedly and permanently existing, and, if changes occur, regards them as mere quantitative increase or decrease, or mere displacement, attributing the cause of such change to factors outside the things rather than within them. Metaphysics holds that a thing can only keep repeating itself, cannot turn into anything different; changes in society in the past thousands of years and changes in nature are of the same kind, only repeating themselves. It attributes the causes of social change to factors outside society, such as geographical and climatic conditions. Metaphysics attributes the motive forces of development to factors outside the thing — in short, it denies the internal contradictions of things. In consequence it cannot explain the diversity of things and the qualitative transformation of one thing into another.

所谓形而上学的或庸俗进化论的宇宙观，就是用孤立的、静止的和片面的观点去看世界。这种宇宙观把世界一切事物，一切事物的形态和种类，都看成是永远彼此孤立和永远不变化的。如果说有变化，也只是数量的增减和场所的变更。而这种增减和变更的原因，不在事物的内部而在事物的外部，即是由于外力的推动。形而上学家们说，世界上的事物，从来就是如此的，将来也永远如此。社会的发展只是数百年来的事情，而自然界的变化，则只是简单地重复。他们不能解释事物的质的多样性，不能解释一种质变为他种质的现象。

I (cont.) · 一、两种宇宙观

Contrary to the metaphysical view of the universe, the dialectical-materialist view of the universe advocates studying the development of things from their internal aspects and from their relations with other things — that is, regarding the development of things as the internal, necessary, self-movement of things, with the movement of each thing interconnected and interacting with the other things around it. The fundamental cause of the development of a thing lies not outside the thing but inside it, in the contradictory nature within the thing. All things contain this contradiction within them, which gives rise to their movement and development. This internal contradiction is the fundamental cause of the development of a thing, while the interconnection and interaction between one thing and others are secondary causes.

和形而上学的宇宙观相反，唯物辩证法的宇宙观主张从事物的内部、从一事物对他事物的关系去研究事物的发展，即把事物的发展看做是事物内部的必然的自己的运动，而每一事物的运动都和它的周围其他事物互相联系着和互相影响着。事物发展的根本原因，不是在事物的外部而是在事物的内部，在于事物内部的矛盾性。任何事物内部都有这种矛盾性，因此引起了事物的运动和发展。事物内部的这种矛盾性是事物发展的根本原因，一事物和他事物的互相联系和互相影响则是事物发展的第二位的原因。

I (cont.) · 一、两种宇宙观

Does dialectical materialism exclude external causes? Not at all. External causes are the conditions of change; internal causes are the basis of change; external causes operate through internal causes. Without eggs, no chicken; without the appropriate temperature, no hatching. The same applies to the relations between nations and individuals. The influence of a world era or a particular environment on a nation or individual operates through the internal contradictions within that nation or individual. The old school of vulgar-evolutionists held that the development of the world and society is entirely due to geographical and climatic conditions; in the present era, some also attribute social development to technology, tools, etc. They are wrong: they cannot explain qualitative change.

唯物辩证法是否排除外部的原因呢？并不排除。唯物辩证法认为外因是变化的条件，内因是变化的根据，外因通过内因而起作用。鸡蛋因得适当的温度而变化为鸡子，但温度不能使石头变为鸡子，因为二者的根据是不同的。两军相争，一胜一败，所以胜败，皆决于内因。胜者或因其强，或因其指挥无误；败者或因其弱，或因其指挥失当，外因通过内因而起作用。一九二七年中国大资产阶级战败了无产阶级，是通过中国无产阶级内部（共产党内部的）的机会主义而起作用的。当着我们清算了这种机会主义的时候，中国革命就重新发展了。后来，中国革命又受了敌人的严重打击，是因为我们党内产生了冒险主义。当着我们清算了这种冒险主义的时候，我们的事业就又重新发展了。由此看来，一个政党要引导革命到胜利，必须依靠自己政治路线的正确和组织上的巩固。

I (cont.) · 一、两种宇宙观

This dialectical conception of the universe teaches us mainly to be good at observing and analyzing the contradictory movement in all things and, on the basis of such analysis, to point out the methods for resolving contradictions. Therefore, a concrete understanding of the law of contradiction in things is very important to us.

这个辩证法的宇宙观，主要地就是教导人们要善于去观察和分析各种事物的矛盾的运动，并根据这种分析，指出解决矛盾的方法。因此，具体地了解事物矛盾这一个法则，对于我们是非常重要的。

II · 二、矛盾的普遍性

The question of the universality or absoluteness of contradiction has a twofold meaning. One is that contradiction exists in the process of development of all things; the other is that in the process of development of each thing, the movement of opposites exists from beginning to end.

矛盾的普遍性或绝对性这个问题有两方面的意义。其一是说，矛盾存在于一切事物的发展过程中；其二是说，每一事物的发展过程中存在着自始至终的矛盾运动。

II (cont.) · 二、矛盾的普遍性

Engels said: "Motion itself is a contradiction." Lenin defined the law of the unity of opposites as "the recognition (discovery) of the mutually exclusive, opposite tendencies in all natural (spiritual and social included) phenomena and processes." These statements are correct. The contradictory aspects of things cannot exist in isolation. If one side of a contradiction, the opposite side which opposes it, did not exist, then the conditions for the existence of that side itself would be lost.

恩格斯说："运动本身就是矛盾。"列宁对于对立统一法则所下的定义，说它就是"承认（发现）自然界（精神和社会两者在内）的一切现象和过程都含有互相矛盾、互相排斥、互相对立的趋向"。这些意见是对的吗？是对的。一切矛盾着的各方面，不能孤立地存在。假如没有和它作对的矛盾的一方，它自己这一方也就失去了存在的条件。

II (cont.) · 二、矛盾的普遍性

In mechanics, action and reaction; in physics, positive and negative electricity; in chemistry, the combination and dissociation of atoms; in social science, class struggle; in war, attack and defense, advance and retreat, victory and defeat — all these contradictory aspects are united in a single thing, and without their opposite aspect, none of them could exist. ... All contradictory things are interconnected; not only do they coexist in a unity under certain conditions, but they also transform themselves into each other under certain conditions — this is the full meaning of the identity of opposites.

试想一切矛盾着的事物或人们心中矛盾着的概念，任何一方面能够独立地存在吗？没有构成某一事物的矛盾和矛盾着的各个方面，它就不能存在。没有生，死就不见；没有死，生也不见。没有上，无所谓下；没有下，也无所谓上。没有祸，无所谓福；没有福，也无所谓祸。没有顺利，无所谓困难；没有困难，也无所谓顺利。没有地主，就没有佃农；没有佃农，也就没有地主。没有资产阶级，就没有无产阶级；没有无产阶级，也就没有资产阶级。没有帝国主义的民族压迫，就没有殖民地和半殖民地；没有殖民地和半殖民地，也就没有帝国主义的民族压迫。一切对立的成分都是这样，因一定的条件，一面互相对立，一面又互相联结、互相贯通、互相渗透、互相依赖，这种性质，叫做同一性。一切矛盾着的方面都因一定条件具备着不同一性，所以称为矛盾。然而又具备着同一性，所以互相联结。

II (cont.) · 二、矛盾的普遍性

Lenin once quoted Goethe: "Everything in heaven and on earth is alive in contradiction." ... The coexistence and struggle of opposites, and their transformation into each other under certain conditions, run through the whole process of the development of things. Contradiction exists in the process of development of all things and runs through the whole process of the development of each thing from beginning to end — this is the universality and absoluteness of contradiction.

列宁常引歌德的话："在一切规定的现象中，都含有矛盾。" ... 矛盾存在于一切事物的发展过程中，矛盾贯串于每一事物发展过程的始终，这是矛盾的普遍性和绝对性。否认事物的矛盾就是否认了一切。这是共通的道理，古今中外，概莫能外。所以它是共性，是绝对性。然而这个共性，即包含于一切个性之中，无个性即无共性。假如除去一切个性，还有什么共性呢？因为矛盾的各各特殊，所以造成了个性。一切个性都是有条件地暂时地存在的，所以是相对的。

III · 三、矛盾的特殊性

Concerning the particularity of contradiction, we must first understand that the division of the sciences is determined by the particular contradictions inherent in their objects. Therefore, the study of a particular contradiction peculiar to a certain field of phenomena constitutes the object of a particular science. For example, positive and negative numbers in mathematics, action and reaction in mechanics, positive and negative electricity in physics, dissociation and combination in chemistry, productive forces and relations of production, class against class, in social science, offense and defense in military science, idealism and materialism, the metaphysical and dialectical views, in philosophy — all these constitute the objects of different sciences precisely because they have particular contradictions and particular essences.

矛盾的特殊性。关于矛盾的特殊性问题，马克思和恩格斯，列宁和斯大林，他们都曾经对于各种不同质的矛盾作过具体的分析。列宁指出："对于矛盾的特殊性，……各地的情形不同，过程的发展也不同。" ... 科学研究的区分，就是根据科学对象所具有的特殊的矛盾性。因此，对于某一现象的领域所特有的某一种矛盾的研究，就构成某一门科学的对象。例如，数学中的正数和负数，机械学中的作用和反作用，物理学中的阴电和阳电，化学中的化分和化合，社会科学中的生产力和生产关系、阶级和阶级的互相斗争，军事学中的攻击和防御，哲学中的唯心论和唯物论、形而上学观和辩证法观等等，都是因为具有特殊的矛盾和特殊的本质，才构成了不同的科学研究的对象。

III (cont.) · 三、矛盾的特殊性

To understand the universality of contradiction is not enough; one must also study its particularity. Unless one studies the particularity of contradiction, one cannot determine the particular essence of a thing that distinguishes it from other things, cannot discover the particular causes or basis of the movement and development of a thing, cannot distinguish things, and cannot delimit the fields of scientific research.

固然，如果不认识矛盾的普遍性，就无从发现事物运动发展的普遍的原因或普遍的根据；但是如果不研究矛盾的特殊性，就无从确定一事物不同于他事物的特殊的本质，就无从发现事物运动发展的特殊的原因，或特殊的根据，也就无从辨别事物，无从区分科学研究的领域。

III (cont.) · 三、矛盾的特殊性

As regards the order of human cognition, men always first come to know the individual and particular nature of many different things, and only then can they proceed to generalization and know the common nature of things. After coming to know this common nature, men use such knowledge as a guide to continue their study of the various concrete things that have not yet been studied or not yet studied in depth, finding their particular essences; only in this way can the cognition of the common nature be supplemented, enriched, and developed. This involves two processes of cognition: one from the particular to the general, and one from the general to the particular. Human cognition always proceeds in such cycles, and each cycle (provided it follows strict scientific method) may advance human cognition one step. The error of our dogmatists on this question is that, on the one hand, they do not understand that one must study the particularity of contradiction and know the particular essence of individual things before one can fully know the universality of contradiction and the common essence of things; on the other hand, they do not understand that after we have come to know the common essence of things, we must continue to study the concrete things that have not yet been deeply studied or newly arisen. They are lazy people who refuse to undertake any arduous study of concrete things, regarding general truth as something that appears out of thin air, turning it into a purely abstract formula that cannot be grasped.

就人类认识运动的秩序说来，总是由认识个别的和特殊的事物，逐步地扩大到认识一般的事物。人们总是首先认识了许多不同事物的特殊的本质，然后才有可能更进一步地进行概括工作，认识诸种事物的共同的本质。当着人们已经认识了这种共同的本质以后，就以这种共同的认识为指导，继续地向着尚未研究过的或者尚未深入地研究过的各种具体的事物进行研究，找出其特殊的本质，这样才可以补充、丰富和发展这种共同的本质的认识，而使这种共同的本质的认识不致变成枯槁的和僵死的东西。这是两个认识的过程：一个是由特殊到一般，一个是由一般到特殊。人类的认识总是这样循环往复地进行的，而每一次的循环（只要是严格地按照科学的方法）都可能使人类的认识提高一步，使人类的认识不断地深化。我们的教条主义者在这个问题上的错误，就是：一方面，不懂得必须研究矛盾的特殊性，认识个别事物的特殊的本质，才有可能充分地认识矛盾的普遍性，充分地认识诸种事物的共同的本质；另一方面，不懂得在我们认识了事物的共同的本质以后，还必须继续研究那些尚未深入地研究过的或者新冒出来的具体的事物。我们的教条主义者是懒汉，他们拒绝对于具体事物做任何艰苦的研究工作，他们把一般真理看成是凭空出现的东西，把它变成为人们所不能够捉摸的纯粹抽象的公式，完全否认了并且颠倒了这个人类认识真理的正常秩序。他们也不懂得人类认识的两个过程的互相联结——由特殊到一般，又由一般到特殊，他们完全不懂得马克思主义的认识论。

III (cont.) · 三、矛盾的特殊性

Not only must we study the particular contradictions and the essences determined by them in each great system of the forms of motion of matter, but we must also study the particular contradictions and essences of each process in the long course of development of each form of motion of matter. Every real, non-fabricated process of development of every form of motion is qualitatively different. What must be specially noted is that the fundamental contradiction and the essence determined by it in the development of a thing do not disappear until the process is completed; but the circumstances at the various stages of the long course of development of a thing often differ from one another. This is because, although the nature of the fundamental contradiction in the process remains unchanged, the fundamental contradiction is progressively intensified at each stage; furthermore, of the many major and minor contradictions determined or influenced by the fundamental contradiction, some are intensified, some are temporarily or partially resolved or mitigated, and some new ones emerge. Hence the process is marked by stages. If one does not pay attention to the stages in the process of development of a thing, one cannot deal appropriately with its contradictions.

不但要研究每一个大系统的物质运动形式的特殊的矛盾性及其所规定的本质，而且要研究每一个物质运动形式在其发展长途中的每一个过程的特殊的矛盾及其本质。一切运动形式的每一个实在的非臆造的发展过程，都是不同质的。我们的研究工作必须着重这一点，而且必须从这一点开始。不同质的矛盾，只有用不同质的方法才能解决。例如，无产阶级和资产阶级的矛盾，用社会主义革命的方法去解决；人民大众和封建制度的矛盾，用民主革命的方法去解决；殖民地和帝国主义的矛盾，用民族民主革命的方法去解决；在社会主义社会中工人阶级和农民阶级的矛盾，用农业集体化和农业机械化的方法去解决；共产党内的矛盾，用批评和自我批评的方法去解决；社会和自然的矛盾，用发展生产力的方法去解决。过程变化，旧过程和旧矛盾消灭，新过程和新矛盾发生，解决矛盾的方法也因之而不同。... 用不同的方法去解决不同的矛盾，这是马克思列宁主义者必须严格地遵守的一个原则。

III (cont.) · 三、矛盾的特殊性

To know the whole process of the development of a thing, one must know the particularity of contradiction in the various stages of that process of development. ... When we study the particularity of any contradiction — the contradiction in each form of motion of matter, in each process of development of each form, in the two aspects of each contradiction in each process, in the development of each contradiction at each stage — we must not be subjective or arbitrary, but must analyze it concretely. Without concrete analysis, there can be no knowledge of the particularity of any contradiction. We must always remember Lenin's words: concrete analysis of concrete conditions.

不但事物发展的全过程中的矛盾运动，在其相互联结上，在其各方情况上，我们必须注意其特点，而且在过程发展的各个阶段中，也有其特点，也必须注意。事物发展过程的根本矛盾及为此根本矛盾所规定的过程的本质，非到过程完结之日，是不会消灭的；但是事物发展的长过程中的各个发展的阶段，情形又往往互相区别。这是因为事物发展过程的根本矛盾的性质和过程的本质虽然没有变化，但是根本矛盾在长过程中的各个发展阶段上采取了逐渐激化的形式。并且，被根本矛盾所规定或影响的许多大小矛盾中，有些是激化了，有些是暂时地或局部地解决了，或者缓和了，又有些是发生了，因此，过程就显出阶段性来。如果人们不去注意事物发展过程中的阶段性，人们就不能适当地处理事物的矛盾。

III (cont.) · 三、矛盾的特殊性

Our dogmatists violate Lenin's instruction. They never analyze any problem concretely; in their articles and speeches they always only mouth the phrase "unity of opposites," without studying the concrete state of opposites, without studying the concrete circumstances of their mutual relation, and without analyzing the concrete conditions under which they transform into each other. ... This is the method of the "Left" opportunists in the period of the First Revolutionary Civil War and the dogmatists during the War of Resistance: both, when dealing with problems, do not proceed from concrete analysis, and hence both are wrong.

我们的教条主义者违背列宁的指示，从来不用脑筋具体地分析任何事物，做起文章或演说来，总是空洞无物的八股调，在我们党内造成了一种极坏的作风。... 这是研究矛盾的特殊性和相对性的问题所必须反对的教条主义的方法。我们从事中国革命的人，不但要在各个矛盾的总体上，即矛盾的相互联结上，了解其特殊性，而且只有从矛盾的各个方面着手研究，才有可能了解其总体。所谓了解矛盾的各个方面，就是了解它们每一方面各占何等特定的地位，各用何种具体形式和对方发生互相依存又互相矛盾的关系，在互相依存又互相矛盾中，以及依存破裂后，又各用何种具体的方法和对方作斗争。研究这些问题，是十分重要的事情。列宁说：马克思主义的最本质的东西，马克思主义的活的灵魂，就在于具体地分析具体的情况。就是说的这个意思。我们的教条主义者违背列宁的指示，从来不用脑筋具体地分析任何事物，做起文章或演说来，总是空洞无物的八股调，在我们党内造成了一种极坏的作风。

IV · 四、主要的矛盾和主要的矛盾方面

In the question of the particularity of contradiction, there are two kinds of circumstances that must be especially singled out for analysis: the principal contradiction and the principal aspect of a contradiction. In the development of a complex thing there are many contradictions, of which one is necessarily the principal one; its existence and development determine or influence the existence and development of the other contradictions.

在矛盾特殊性的问题中，还有两种情形必须特别地提出来加以分析，这就是主要的矛盾和主要的矛盾方面。在复杂的事物的发展过程中，有许多的矛盾存在，其中必有一种是主要的矛盾，由于它的存在和发展，规定或影响着其他矛盾的存在和发展。

IV (cont.) · 四、主要的矛盾和主要的矛盾方面

Thus, if any process involves many contradictions, one of them is necessarily the principal one, playing the leading and decisive role, while the others occupy a secondary and subordinate position. Therefore, in studying any process, if it is a complex process involving two or more contradictions, we must bend every effort to find its principal contradiction. Once this principal contradiction is grasped, all problems can be readily solved. ... This is the method which Marx and Stalin, thousands of revolutionary teachers and leaders, taught us — the method of studying the great importance of the principal contradiction.

由此可知，任何过程如果是有多数矛盾存在的话，其中必定有一种是主要的，起着领导的、决定的作用，其他则处于次要和服从的地位。因此，研究任何过程，如果是存在着两个以上矛盾的复杂过程的话，就要用全力找出它的主要矛盾。捉住了这个主要矛盾，一切问题就迎刃而解了。... 这是马克思和斯大林、几千个革命导师和领袖教给我们的方法。我们从事中国革命的人，不但要在各个矛盾的总体上，即矛盾的相互联结上，了解其特殊性，而且只有从矛盾的各个方面着手研究，才有可能了解其总体。

IV (cont.) · 四、主要的矛盾和主要的矛盾方面

We must not treat all the contradictions in a process as equal, but must distinguish them into the principal and the secondary, and concentrate on grasping the principal one. At the same time, in studying the two aspects of any contradiction, we must not treat them equally, but must distinguish the principal aspect from the secondary aspect and grasp the principal aspect. In the development of a thing, the two contradictory aspects are at once united and struggling; if one aspect gains the dominant position, the nature of the thing changes accordingly.

不能把过程中所有的矛盾平均看待，必须把它们区别为主要的和次要的两类，着重于捉住主要的矛盾，已如上述。但是在各种矛盾之中，不论是主要的或次要的，矛盾着的两个方面，又是否可以平均看待呢？也是不可以的。无论什么矛盾，矛盾的诸方面，其发展是不平衡的。有时候似乎势均力敌，然而这只是暂时的和相对的情形，基本的形态则是不平衡。矛盾着的两方面中，必有一方面是主要的，他方面是次要的。其主要的方面，即所谓矛盾起主导作用的方面。事物的性质，主要地是由取得支配地位的矛盾的主要方面所规定的。

IV (cont.) · 四、主要的矛盾和主要的矛盾方面

However, this situation is not fixed: the principal and non-principal aspects of a contradiction transform into each other, and the nature of the thing changes accordingly. ... We often speak of "the new replacing the old." Metabolism is the universal and irresistible law of the universe. The internal contradictions of any thing form a series of tortuous struggles between the new and the old. The result of the struggle is that the new aspect grows from small to big, rising to the dominant position, while the old aspect declines from big to small, gradually moving toward extinction. Once the new aspect gains dominance over the old, the nature of the old thing changes into that of the new thing. This shows that the nature of a thing is determined principally by the principal aspect of the dominant contradiction; once this aspect changes, the nature of the thing changes accordingly.

然而这种情形不是固定的，矛盾的主要和非主要的方面互相转化着，事物的性质也就随着起变化。在矛盾发展的一定过程或一定阶段上，主要方面属于甲方，非主要方面属于乙方；到了另一发展阶段或另一发展过程时，就互易其位置，这是依靠事物发展中矛盾双方斗争的力量的增减程度来决定的。我们常常说"新陈代谢"这句话。新陈代谢是宇宙间普遍的永远不可抵抗的规律。任何事物的内部都有其新旧两个方面的矛盾，形成为一系列的曲折的斗争。斗争的结果，新的方面由小变大，上升为支配的东西；旧的方面则由大变小，变成逐步归于灭亡的东西。而一当新的方面对于旧的方面取得支配地位的时候，旧事物的性质就变化为新事物的性质。由此可见，事物的性质主要地是由取得支配地位的矛盾的主要方面所规定的。取得支配地位的矛盾的主要方面起了变化，事物的性质也就随着起变化。

IV (cont.) · 四、主要的矛盾和主要的矛盾方面

The study of the various states of imbalance in contradiction — of the principal and non-principal contradictions, of the principal and non-principal aspects of contradictions — is an important method for a revolutionary political party to correctly determine its strategic and tactical principles in politics and military affairs, and one that all Communists must pay attention to.

对于矛盾的各种不平衡情况的研究，对于主要的矛盾和非主要的矛盾、主要的矛盾方面和非主要的矛盾方面的研究，成为革命政党正确地决定其政治上和军事上的战略战术方针的重要方法之一，是一切共产党人都应当注意的。

V · 五、矛盾诸方面的同一性和斗争性

Identity, unity, coincidence, interpenetration, interpermeation, mutual dependence (or mutual support), mutual connection, or mutual cooperation — these different terms all mean the same thing and refer to the following two situations: first, each of the two aspects of every contradiction in the development of a thing presupposes the existence of the aspect opposed to it as the condition for its own existence, and both sides coexist in a unity; second, under certain conditions, each of the contradictory aspects transforms itself into its opposite. This is what is meant by identity.

同一性、统一性、一致性、互相渗透、互相贯通、互相依赖（或依存）、互相联结或互相合作，这些不同的名词都是一个意思，说的是如下两种情形：第一、事物发展过程中的每一种矛盾的两个方面，各以和它对立着的方面为自己存在的前提，双方共处于一个统一体中；第二、矛盾着的双方，依据一定的条件，各向着其相反的方面转化。这些就是所谓同一性。

V (cont.) · 五、矛盾诸方面的同一性和斗争性

Lenin said: "Dialectics is the doctrine which shows how opposites can be and how they happen to be (how they become) identical — under what conditions they are identical, transforming themselves into one another, — why the human mind should not take these opposites as dead and rigid, but as living, conditional, mobile, transforming themselves into one another." ... The contradictory aspects of every thing cannot exist in isolation. Without the other aspect, each aspect loses the condition for its own existence.

列宁说："辩证法是一种学说，它研究对立面怎样才能够同一，是怎样（怎样成为）同一的——在什么条件下它们是相互转化而同一的，——为什么人的头脑不应当把这些对立面看作僵死的、凝固的东西，而应当看作生动的、有条件的、可变动的、互相转化的东西。" ... 矛盾着的各方面，不能孤立地存在。假如没有和它作对的矛盾的一方，它自己这一方就失去了存在的条件。

V (cont.) · 五、矛盾诸方面的同一性和斗争性

All contradictory things are interconnected; not only do they coexist in a unity under certain conditions, but they also transform themselves into each other under certain conditions — this is the full meaning of the identity of opposites. ... The so-called conditional and relative identity means that, without certain conditions, the struggle of the two contradictory aspects cannot transform themselves into each other. ... Identity is conditional and relative; struggle is unconditional and absolute. The combination of conditional, relative identity and unconditional, absolute struggle constitutes the contradictory movement of all things.

一切矛盾着的东西，互相联系着，不但在一定条件之下共处于一个统一体中，而且在一定条件之下互相转化，这就是矛盾的同一性的全部意义。 ... 所谓矛盾在一定条件下的同一性，就是说，没有一定的条件，矛盾着的双方都不会转化。 ... 有条件的相对的同一性和无条件的绝对的斗争性相结合，构成了一切事物的矛盾运动。

V (cont.) · 五、矛盾诸方面的同一性和斗争性

The movement of all things assumes two states: the state of relative rest and the state of conspicuous change. Both kinds of movement are caused by the mutual struggle of the two contradictory factors within things. ... Contradiction and struggle are universal and absolute, but the method of resolving contradictions, that is, the form of struggle, differs according to the different nature of the contradiction. ... Antagonism is one form of the struggle of opposites, but not the only form.

无论什么事物的运动都采取两种状态，相对地静止的状态和显著地变动的状态。两种状态的运动都是由事物内部包含的两个矛盾的因素互相斗争所引起的。当着事物的运动在第一种状态的时候，它只有数量的变化，没有性质的变化，所以显出好似静止的面貌。当着事物的运动在第二种状态的时候，它已由第一种状态中的数量的变化达到了某一个最高点，引起了统一物的分解，发生了性质的变化，所以显出显著地变化的面貌。... 矛盾和斗争是普遍的、绝对的，但是解决矛盾的方法，即斗争的形式，则因矛盾的性质不同而不相同。... 对抗是矛盾斗争的一种形式，而不是矛盾斗争的一切形式。

VI · 六、对抗在矛盾中的地位

Antagonism is one form of the struggle of opposites, but not the only form. We must make a concrete analysis of the circumstances of each particular contradiction. Contradictions in nature, in society, and in the ranks of the revolutionaries and counter-revolutionaries all assume different forms of struggle according to their different natures.

对抗是矛盾斗争的一种形式，而不是矛盾斗争的一切形式。对于矛盾的各种斗争形式，必须作具体的分析。敌对势力之间的矛盾，是对抗性的矛盾；革命队伍内部的矛盾，在正常情形下，是非对抗性的矛盾，但处理不当，也会发展为对抗性的矛盾。

VI (cont.) · 六、对抗在矛盾中的地位

In nature, antagonistic struggle exists between life and death, between the old and the new, and so on. In society, before the elimination of classes, the struggle between classes assumes the form of antagonism. In the Communist Party, the contradiction between correct and incorrect thinking, between proletarian and non-proletarian ideology, takes the form of criticism and self-criticism in normal times — a non-antagonistic form of struggle. Lenin's formulation that antagonism is not universal and that the transition from non-antagonism to antagonism is conditional is correct.

在自然界中，生死、新旧之间的斗争，是对抗性的矛盾。在社会现象中，敌对阶级之间的斗争，是对抗性的矛盾。共产党内的正确思想和错误思想的矛盾，在阶级社会里，是无产阶级思想同非无产阶级思想的矛盾，在社会主义社会里，是无产阶级思想同资产阶级思想、同小资产阶级思想的矛盾。党内不同思想的对立和斗争是经常发生的，这是社会的阶级矛盾和新旧事物的矛盾在党内的反映。党内如果没有矛盾和解决矛盾的思想斗争，党的生命也就停止了。因此，我们党采取批评和自我批评的方法，进行马克思列宁主义思想教育，借以解决党内矛盾，使党团结和统一。

VII · 七、结论

We may now sum up briefly. The law of the contradiction in things, that is, the law of the unity of opposites, is the fundamental law of nature and of society, and hence also the fundamental law of thought. It stands opposed to the metaphysical conception of the universe. It represents a great revolution in the history of human knowledge. In the view of dialectical materialism, contradiction exists in all objective things and in all subjective thought; contradiction runs through all processes from beginning to end — this is the universality and absoluteness of contradiction. Each contradictory thing and each aspect of it has its own characteristics — this is the particularity and relativity of contradiction. Contradictory things have identity under certain conditions and hence can coexist in a unity and transform themselves into each other — this is again the particularity and relativity of contradiction. But the struggle of opposites is uninterrupted; it exists at all times, whether during their coexistence or during their transformation, and becomes especially conspicuous during transformation — this is again the universality and absoluteness of contradiction. When we study the particularity and relativity of contradiction, we must pay attention to the universality and absoluteness of contradiction and of the struggle of opposites; and when we study the universality and absoluteness of contradiction, we must pay attention to the particularity and relativity of contradiction. ... In studying a problem, we must not be subjective or arbitrary; we must proceed from concrete analysis. We must always remember Lenin's words: concrete analysis of concrete conditions.

说到这里，我们可以总起来说几句。事物矛盾的法则，即对立统一的法则，是自然和社会的根本法则，因而也是思维的根本法则。它是和形而上学的宇宙观相反的。它对于人类的认识史是一个大革命。按照辩证唯物论的观点看来，矛盾存在于一切客观事物和主观思维的过程中，矛盾贯串于一切过程的始终，这是矛盾的普遍性和绝对性。矛盾着的事物及其每一个侧面各有其特点，这是矛盾的特殊性和相对性。矛盾着的事物依一定的条件有同一性，因此能够共居于一个统一体中，又能够互相转化到相反的方面去，这又是矛盾的特殊性和相对性。然而矛盾的斗争则是不断的，不管在它们共居的时候，或者在它们互相转化的时候，都有斗争的存在，尤其是在它们互相转化的时候，斗争的表现更为显著，这又是矛盾的普遍性和绝对性。当着我们研究矛盾的特殊性和相对性的时候，要注意矛盾和斗争方面的普遍性和绝对性；当着我们研究矛盾的普遍性和斗争性的时候，要注意矛盾的各种运动形态的特殊性。 ... 离开具体的分析，就不能认识任何矛盾的特性。我们必须时刻记得列宁的话：对于具体的事物作具体的分析。

VII (cont.) · 七、结论

This concrete analysis of concrete conditions is the living soul of Marxism. ... Our dogmatists, however, are the very people who violate this instruction, never analyzing concrete conditions, but always empty phrases. ... The dialectical-materialist law of the unity of opposites, with the further development of practice and cognition, will certainly continue to be enriched and developed.

这种具体的分析，马克思和恩格斯、列宁和斯大林都给了我们以光辉的模范。我们的教条主义者，恰恰是这个活的灵魂所最不能容的人。他们从来不用脑筋具体地分析任何事物，做起文章或演说来，总是空洞无物的八股调。... 事物矛盾的法则，即对立统一的法则，是自然和社会的根本法则，因而也是思维的根本法则。随着实践和认识的发展，唯物辩证法的这一根本法则，必将愈加丰富，愈加发展。

AI&Philosophy January 15, 2026

On Protracted War

Mao Zedong's "On Protracted War" (论持久战), a series of lectures delivered in May 1938 at the Yenan Association for the Study of the Anti-Japanese War, presented in full in bilingual layout — English on the left, Chinese on the right.

By Mao Zedong
1 · 一、问题的提起

Ten months of experience in the War of Resistance have proved the error of the following two views: the theory of national subjugation and the theory of quick victory. The former gives rise to the tendency to compromise; the latter to the tendency to underestimate the enemy. Both approach the problem subjectively and one-sidedly — in a word, unscientifically.

抗战十个月以来，一切经验都证明下述两种观点的不对：一种是中国必亡论，一种是中国速胜论。前者产生妥协倾向，后者产生轻敌倾向。他们看问题的方法都是主观的和片面的，一句话，非科学的。

1 (cont.) · 一、问题的提起

Before the war, there was much loose talk of national subjugation... Some people said, "China's arms are inferior, so we are bound to lose." Later, after the fighting began at Shanghai, Nankou, Hsinkou, and other places, the opposite was said: "China can win in three days; the war will be over in three months."... Ten months of experience have refuted both.

抗战以前，存在着许多亡国论的议论。他们说："中国武器不如人，战必败。"抗战开始以后，又有一种议论，说什么"只要打三个月，国际局势一定变化，苏联一定出兵，战争就可解决" ... 台儿庄胜利之后，有些人主张徐州战役应是"准决战"，说过去的持久战方针应该改变。平型关一个胜仗，冲昏了一些人的头脑；台儿庄再一个胜仗，冲昏了更多的人的头脑。于是敌人是否进攻武汉，成为疑问了。许多人以为："不一定"；许多人以为："断不会" ... 抗战十个月以来，种种事实，驳斥了这些议论。

1 (cont.) · 一、问题的提起

This war is unprecedented in Eastern history, and will also be great in world history. All the four great characteristics — our war is just, the enemy's is unjust; the enemy is strong in some respects but weak in others; we are weak in some respects but strong in others; the enemy has little international support while we have much — together determine and regulate all political and military strategy on both sides, and the protracted nature of the war. The war will be a contest of these characteristics. They exist in fact, are not invented; they are all the basic elements of the war, not fragments; they permeate all big and small questions on both sides and all stages of operations.

抗战十个月以来，各种表现轻重不同的中国必亡论和中国速胜论，其最主要的原因，就在于他们看问题的方法是主观的和片面的。... 中日战争不是任何别的战争，乃是半殖民地半封建的中国和帝国主义的日本之间在二十世纪三十年代进行的一个决死的战争。全部问题的根据就在这里。分别地说来，战争的双方有如下互相反对的许多特点。日本方面：第一，它是一个强的帝国主义国家，它的军力、经济力和政治组织力在东方是一等的，在世界也是五六个著名帝国主义国家中的一个。这是日本侵略战争的基本条件，战争的不可避免和中国的不能速胜，就建立在这个日本国家的帝国主义制度及其强的军力、经济力和政治组织力上面。然而第二，由于日本社会经济的帝国主义性，就产生了日本战争的帝国主义性，它的战争是退步的和野蛮的。... 第三，日本国度比较地小，其人力、军力、财力、物力均感缺乏，经不起长期的战争。... 第四，日本发动的是帝国主义战争，它虽然能得到国际法西斯国家的援助，但同时却又不能不遇到一个超过其国际援助力量的国际反对力量。... 中国方面：第一，我们是一个半殖民地半封建的国家... 军力、经济力和政治组织力各方面都显得不如敌人。战争之不可避免和中国之不能速胜，又在这个方面有其基础。然而第二，中国近百年的解放运动积累到了今日，已经不同于任何历史时代。... 中国有了已经觉悟的广大人民，有了共产党，有了政治上进步的军队即共产党领导的人民解放军（按：当时为人民抗日军队）... 第三，中国又是一个很大的国家，地大、物博、人多、兵多，能够支持长期的战争... 第四，由于中国战争的进步性、正义性而产生的国际广大援助，同日本的失道寡助又恰恰相反。... 这些特点，规定了和规定着双方一切政治上的政策和军事上的战略战术，规定了和规定着战争的持久性和最后胜利属于中国而不属于日本。

2 · 二、问题的根据

Let us now examine the basis of these characteristics. The Japanese warlords... control large industry and finance; but Japan's national economy is developing on a narrow base, with a limited population and resources; its war is imperialist and hence reactionary, arousing the opposition of its own people and of the whole world. China, though weak in military, economic, and political organizational strength, has the most fundamental of advantages: a progressive and just war, an awakened people, an enormous territory, and a broad united front.

现在我们来把持久战问题研究一下。... 日本的军力、经济力和政治组织力是强的，但其战争是退步的、野蛮的，人力、财力又不充足，国际形势又处于不利。中国反是，军力、经济力和政治组织力是比较地弱的，然而正处于进步的时代，其战争是进步的和正义的，又有大国这个条件足以支持持久战，世界的多数国家是会要援助中国的。这些，就是中日战争互相矛盾着的基本特点。这些特点，规定了和规定着双方一切政治上的政策和军事上的战略战术，规定了和规定着战争的持久性和最后胜利属于中国而不属于日本。

3 · 三、驳亡国论

The theorists of national subjugation are wrong because they see only the contrast between the enemy's strength and our weakness, and exaggerate this contrast into the criterion of the war; they fail to see that many other conditions counteract this contrast. They forget that Japan, though strong, is small and reactionary, while China, though weak, is large and progressive. The war is a contest of all these conditions; to recognize only one side is unscientific.

亡国论者看重了强弱一个矛盾，把它夸大起来作为全部问题的论据，而忽略了其他的矛盾。他们只提强弱对比一点，是他们的片面性；他们将此片面的东西夸大起来看成全体，又是他们的主观性。所以在全体说来，他们是没有根据的，是错误的。... 他们的思想的枯竭和固定，证明了他们的无能。

4 · 四、妥协还是抗战？腐败还是进步？

Whether compromise or resistance, whether corruption or progress — these are not merely matters of subjective choice; they are determined by the objective conditions of the war. The enemy wants compromise, and has the means to induce it; we want resistance, but there are also elements in our ranks inclined to compromise. The task of the whole nation is to persist in resistance and progress; to eliminate compromise and corruption. Progress and resistance are the only way to final victory.

妥协还是抗战？腐败还是进步？——这是两个基本的问题。... 敌人以诱降和分化来动摇我们的抗战意志，企图实现其不战而屈人之计的幻想。我们则必须坚持抗战、坚持进步、坚持团结。... 政治上、军事上、经济上、教育上的各项进步，是抗战胜利的必要条件。而政治上的进步，尤为其他一切进步的条件和基础。

5 · 五、亡国论是不对的，速胜论也是不对的

Both the theory of national subjugation and the theory of quick victory are wrong. The former underestimates our own strengths and overestimates the enemy's; the latter overestimates our own strengths and underestimates the enemy's. Both see only one side of the matter. The correct conclusion is: the war will be protracted, and the final victory will belong to China.

亡国论是不对的，速胜论也是不对的。... 亡国论者看重了强弱一个矛盾，把它夸大起来作为全部问题的论据；速胜论者则根本忘记了强弱这个矛盾，而记起了其他矛盾。... 他们或者毫无根据地说，敌人如何如何不行，中国如何如何了不得；或者把一次两次的胜利，误认为是全局的根本变化。... 我们的结论是：中国会亡吗？答复：不会亡，最后胜利是中国的。中国能够速胜吗？答复：不能速胜，抗日战争是持久战。

6 · 六、为什么是持久战？

Why is the war protracted? Because the enemy is strong but weak in essence, and we are weak but strong in essence. The enemy is strong in military, economic, and political organizational power, so the war cannot be won quickly; we cannot expect quick victory. The enemy's war is reactionary and unjust, its manpower and material resources are insufficient, and its international position is unfavorable; our war is progressive and just, we have a vast territory and a united people, and we enjoy broad international sympathy. Therefore, the final victory will be ours; but because the enemy is strong in the initial balance of power, victory will be protracted.

为什么是持久战？... 中日战争既然是持久战，最后胜利又将是属于中国的，那末，就可以合理地设想，这种持久战，将具体地表现于三个阶段之中。第一个阶段，是敌之战略进攻、我之战略防御的时期。第二个阶段，是敌之战略保守、我之准备反攻的时期。第三个阶段，是我之战略反攻、敌之战略退却的时期。... 持久战，就是将敌人的战略进攻阻止住，消耗敌人，壮大自己，准备反攻，最后把敌人驱逐出境，收复失地，取得胜利。这就是抗日战争发展的全部过程。

7 · 七、持久战的三个阶段

The protracted war will pass through three stages. The first stage is the enemy's strategic offensive and our strategic defense. The second stage is the enemy's strategic conservatism and our preparation for the counteroffensive. The third stage is our strategic counteroffensive and the enemy's strategic retreat. The first stage will be the longest and most trying; in it, the enemy advances and occupies much territory, while we fight mobile and guerrilla warfare, wearing the enemy down. The second stage will be a long period of strategic stalemate on the enemy's side, in which the enemy attempts to hold the occupied territory and we prepare the counteroffensive. The third stage will be the counteroffensive, recovering our lost territory. China's War of Resistance as a whole is protracted, but each of its stages may be long or short, depending on changes in the balance of forces.

持久战的三个阶段：第一阶段，敌之战略进攻、我之战略防御；第二阶段，敌之战略保守、我之准备反攻；第三阶段，我之战略反攻、敌之战略退却。... 第一阶段，现在还没有完结，敌之企图是攻占广州、武汉、兰州三点，并把三点联系起来。... 此阶段中，我军以游击战和运动战为主，消耗敌人，诱敌深入。... 第二阶段，可以名之曰战略的相持阶段。... 此阶段将是敌人最感困难的时候，也是我们准备反攻的时候。... 第三阶段，是收复失地的反攻阶段。... 三个阶段中，第一阶段是最困难的，也是最富于变化的一个阶段。... 战争的胜负，固然决定于双方军事、政治、经济、地理、战争性质、国际援助诸条件，然而不仅仅决定于这些；仅有这些，还只是有了胜负的可能性，它本身没有分胜负。要分胜负，还须加上主观的努力，这就是指导战争和实行战争，这就是战争中的自觉的能动性。

8 · 八、犬牙交错的战争

Because of the special conditions of China's War of Resistance — a large, weak country fighting a small, strong one — the war assumes a jigsaw-like pattern: within the overall interior-line operations there are exterior-line operations; within strategic defense there are tactical offensives; within protracted warfare there are quick decisions; within mobile warfare there are positional and guerrilla warfare; the front and the rear interlock; large areas are lost and recovered. This interlocking pattern is a distinctive feature of the war, and it is precisely this pattern that gives China the possibility of protracted resistance and final victory.

犬牙交错的战争，是持久战的一个特点。... 内线和外线——抗日战争是整个处于内线作战的地位的；但是主力军和游击队之间，主力军在内线，游击队在外线，形成内外夹攻。防御和进攻——抗日战争，就全体上说来，是战略防御的；但在战役上，却是进攻的。持久和速决——抗日战争是持久战，但具体的战役战斗，却要求速决。... 包围和反包围——从整个战争看来，由于敌之战略进攻和外线作战，我处战略防御和内线作战地位，无疑我是被敌包围的。... 但是，若将各个游击根据地联系起来看，并将各个游击根据地和正规军的阵地也联系起来看，我又把许多敌人都包围起来。... 大块和小块——敌占地区，实际上占去了中国的大块地方，然而还有小块的地方未被敌占。... 有退有进——在敌人进攻之下，我军有大块的丧失；但在敌后，我又有大块的恢复和建立。... 这种犬牙交错的战争形态，是抗日战争区别于其他战争的一个特点，是持久战的胜利的一个条件。

9 · 九、为永久和平而战

The Chinese people's War of Resistance is a war for permanent peace. ... Imperialism is the source of modern war; to eliminate war, we must first eliminate imperialism. ... The war we are fighting is a part of the world's struggle for peace; our victory will contribute to the peace of the world. ... China's war of resistance is not merely a war of national liberation, but also a part of the struggle for the liberation of all mankind. For only by defeating Japanese imperialism can we open the way to permanent peace for all nations.

为永久和平而战。... 我们的战争是神圣的、正义的、进步的，求和平。... 人类的高贵性的战争的胜利，将给日本的侵略战争以致命的打击，将使世界和平得到保障。... 我们的抗日战争，包含着为争取永久和平而战的性质。... 历史上的战争分为两类，一类是正义的，一类是非正义的。一切进步的战争都是正义的，一切阻碍进步的战争都是非正义的。... 我们从事战争的信念，便建筑在争取永久和平这个伟大的目标上面。

10 · 十、能动性在战争中

We now speak of the "conscious dynamic role" in war. ... War is a contest of the material conditions of the two sides; but the outcome of the contest is not decided by material conditions alone. The direction and outcome of the war are decided by the subjective efforts of men on both sides, by their strategy and tactics, their ability to direct and carry on the war. We hold that correct subjective direction can transform disadvantage into advantage and passivity into initiative; incorrect subjective direction can do the reverse. The issue of war is decided by the conscious dynamic role of men, within the limits permitted by objective conditions. Everything that man does depends on his correct understanding of objective laws and his ability to act upon them.
